When assessing the actions of Poland within the European Union, we cannot ignore the efforts of our government to adopt favourable decisions in the field of climate policy for the Polish economy. The European Commission's efforts to reduce carbon dioxide emissions at all costs, regardless of the energy specificities of the Member States, can have a negative impact on the Polish economy. Polish conventional power plants are taking a number of measures to reduce greenhouse gas emissions, but they are not able to achieve in a short time the indicators expected by the European Commission. It is therefore very important to adopt rational decisions at European Union level, taking into account the possibility of using natural resources held by individual countries. The Polish government is consistently aiming to adopt such regulations. Similarly, our country's efforts to take decisive action on the Russian-Ukrainian conflict should be positively assessed. The Polish government clearly indicated the negative impact of the existing situation on the stability of the Eastern European area. He also raised the need to resume strategic communication in connection with the increasing Russian propaganda towards the consistently led information war by the Kremlin, as the European Union remains practically passive. The European Union even supports some of President Vladimir Putin's actions, e.g. the gas pipeline case Nord Stream 2. Russia does not hide that the opening of this pipeline will result in the cessation of gas supply to Western Europe through the territory of Ukraine. Poland, the Baltic States and Ukraine oppose this project. It poses a threat to the energy security of these countries, especially Poland, because it can block the use of the gas terminal in Świnoujście due to the running bottom of the Baltic Sea pipeline Nord Stream 2. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-12-sejm-49-3.u472	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! I present the position of the Poselski Club Kukiz'15 in relation to the law on amending the law on packaging and packaging waste management and certain other laws. Any amendments made to Chapter 6 of the Law of 13 June 2013 on the management of packaging and packaging waste are necessary from the point of view of the state's activities and international restrictions. However, this law is a consequence of trends in the EU institutions, which we are obliged to implement as Poland. It has long been known that plastic bags in landfills are not a threat to the environment, but are abandoned in forests and ditches. Therefore, there are several controversial issues. In Article 2, paragraph 2 is replaced by the following: Points 1 and 2 of the 40a include the provision of a recycling fee. The fee is charged for light foil bags, not very light foil bags. From the point of view of the decay period, very light foil bags and lightweight foil bags are made of similar materials. For the environment, very light plastic bags, which are one-off than the thicker ones that can be used several times, create a greater threat. The introduction of a maximum recycling charge of PLN 1 per lightweight plastic bag according to free market rights means an increase in prices for lightweight plastic bags. In other words, if a customer wants to buy vegetables from a farmer in retail form, he will have to buy expensive commercials or use his own. If the degree of environmental impact is combined with the average possibility of using foil bags, it turns out that the production of even paper or material bags is more negative. In line with its policy, the EU is trying to put pressure on Member States to reduce the production of lightweight plastic bags. These actions are driven by the belief that the period of distribution of such a bag may endanger the environment. However, according to our assessment, the introduction of restrictions on lightweight plastic bags is completely unnecessary. The correct process of burning the foil waste with the filters on allows you to completely dispose of light foil bags while generating heat energy. In conclusion, the foil bag due to its chemical composition, applied technologies and properly used filters can be safely burned without any adverse environmental impact. Instead of forcing the consumer with additional taxes to give up comfortable, lightweight plastic bags for often more environmentally harmful substitutes, it would suffice to introduce amenities for ecotoreb producers and their customers. Reaching citizens' pockets without a proposal for equal replacements is a bad idea. Poland is forced to adopt this law, but this does not change the fact that the costs will be borne by simple consumers shopping in home shops. Possibly, after the introduction of this law, foreign trade networks will introduce eco-torks made according to Western technology. Thank you. Question. Many companies complain about the high cost of recovery from some packages. Allowing the market to be environmentally harmful and difficult to recycle packaging, which is often more expensive than their environmental substitutes, generates additional losses for the state and households. Extortion of only organic and easy-to-recyclable packaging on manufacturers will reduce the amount of waste remaining on landfills. When does the Ministry plan to introduce real regulations for certain packages that are difficult to recycle?

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-12-sejm-49-3.u476	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present the resolution of the Polish Sejm on the establishment of 2018 with the Year of Remembrance of the Wielkopolska Uprising. [[The Gathered Rise Up]] The Victory of the Wielkopolska Uprising 1918-1919 has been a great crowning point for 'the longest war of modern Europe' - the great triumph of Poles seeking liberation from the Prussian captivity, which was inherently accompanied by a desire to reunite with the beloved Commonwealth. The solid foundation of the independence efforts of Wielkopolska became such qualities as ardent patriotism, unhindered work at the foundations and authentic social spirit. They are titans of organic work, among them among others. Gen. Dezydery Chłapowski, Dr. Karol Marcinkowski, Emilia Sczaniecka, Hipolit Cegielski, Fr. Piotr Wawrzyniak or Maximilian Jackowski, through their social, cultural, economic and scientific activities, raising generations of educated, aware of their Polishness, contributed greatly to the subsequent success of the Wielkopolska Uprising. It is thanks to the heroic uprising of the insurgents of Wielkopolska 1918-1919, headed by the creators of the Wielkopolska Army, Major Stanisław Taczak and General Józef Dowbor-Muśnicki, their determination and generous blood donation almost all of Wielkopolska was incorporated back into the reborn Republic of Poland. The contribution of the inhabitants of Wielkopolska to the insurgent struggles in Upper Silesia and the commitment of the well-equipped and trained Wielkopolska Army to defending the independence of the reborn Homeland against the Bolshevik attack in 1920 cannot be overlooked either by silence. When the armed struggle was over, the inhabitants of Wielkopolska took the task of rebuilding and reuniting the Republic, destroyed by the historical turmoil. In this way they creatively referred to a rich positivistic tradition - the heritage of the Founders' Fathers of modern Wielkopolska - because there was a time when 'Poland needed sweat, not blood'. Bearing in mind the contribution of many generations of Greater Poland to the struggle for independence of the Republic, and then its reconstruction and the significant impact of the victorious Wielkopolska Uprising 1918-1919 on the shape of the borders of the reborn Polish State, the Polish Sejm establishes 2018 as the Year of the Remembrance of the Greater Poland Uprising. [[The Gathered Rise Up]] Resolution of the Polish Parliament on the commemoration of Tadeusz Kościuszko on the 200th anniversary of death On 15 October 2017, we celebrate the 200th anniversary of the death of Tadeusz Kościuszko - a general of the Polish army and the American army, leader of Insurection from 1794, a participant in the war for independence of the United States, a tireless freedom fighter. Tadeusz Kościuszko occupies a special place in the pantheon of national heroes. In recognition of his excellent command skills and bravery, he was honored with the Order of Virtuti Militari. His ideals are timeless and are still a model of patriotic attitudes and service to the State. Appreciating these values of the Polish Sejm, the resolution of 22 June 2016 established the year 2017 - the Year of Tadeusz Kościuszko. For Poles and other nationalities General Tadeusz Kościuszko is a symbol of freedom and courage. The Sejm of the Republic of Poland honors the memory of Chief Tadeusz Kościuszko and expresses its highest appreciation for his ideals, his dedication to the Homeland and his political and military achievements. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-12-sejm-49-3.u526	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On September 1, 1977, the school bell rang for the first time at Primary School No. 7 in Chojnice. Today, the school community symbolically celebrated the anniversary of the 40th anniversary of the school's establishment during the ceremonial academy at the school. The decision to start a new primary school in Chojnice was due to the need, necessity. The schools in Chojnice were unable to accommodate all the students of post-war demographic boom in the 1960s. Students learned under terrible conditions. The lessons lasted until late evening hours. After many requests from the municipal authorities addressed to the then decision-makers of the Bydgoszcz Voivodeship in 1975, it was decided to build a new primary school and its location in the Bytowski Estate. Construction of a long-awaited facility has begun, which has been established so-called. the collective municipal school, thanks to which it received the status of the leading municipal institution and became the seat of the municipal education. The school building was opened on 11 November 1976, but only on 1 September 1977 the first pupils appeared at the school. $1,000. students from 31 classes began to study with 38 teachers at a modern school. In the 10th anniversary of the uprising on 11 December 1987, the school received the name of Jan Karnowski, a philanthropist, a poet, but above all a regionalist and a great lover of culture and Kashubian history, animator of cultural life of Chojnice land. During the ceremony, the school received the banner. Until 1988, Primary School No. 7 Jan Karnowski in Chojnice was the largest educational institution in Chojnice. After the reform of education in the school building, School Complex No. 7 was established in Chojnice, which included Junior High School No. 3 and Primary School No. 7. Today the school looks completely different than it did 40 years ago. In 2011, a new gym and Orlik field were established at the school, and classes of zero were opened, and since last year a modern kindergarten has been operating at the school. Her graduates returned to school, who worked as teachers in this institution, and remember the first years of so-called. Sevens. Since the school year 2017/2018, primary school No 7 is again an eight-year-old institution with 933 students, 85 teachers and 32 administrative and service staff. From this point of view, I would like to congratulate the school board, the pedagogical group, the staff and graduates and the present students on the beautiful anniversary of your school. I am very pleased to be able to thank you from the Seym tribune for your great work as a teacher with children. I know how unappreciated this work is, but the satisfaction of doing well in the form of smiling students will be the greatest reward for you. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-12-sejm-49-3.u530	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! In August of this year, the Act of 20 July 2017 amending the Real Estate Management Act entered into force. The legislator provided for changes in the profession of asset valuer. In the amended act, an article was added. 189b, which introduces real estate valuation regulations. On the basis of this article, the person determining the value of the property must be an asset valuer. According to the amended law, a person who does not pursue the profession of asset valuer and determines the value of the property shall be fined up to 50,000. Due to the lack of precise indication of situations in which the valuation of the property is subject only to the property valuer, a number of doubts arose. The problem concerns successions, donations, loans for real estate. In each of the WWs. the situation of the citizen is obliged to indicate the value of the property. So if he typed it himself, e.g. in the contract, the value of the property will be subject to this fine of up to 50,000.? Similar doubts arise in the case of e.g. declarations of property which must be filled by councillors, Members, Senators, School Directors. Any person who completes a statement of property must estimate the value of the property held. So far there has been no requirement for the valuer to measure the property. Following media reports on the estimation of the value of real estate, following these reports, a memo appeared on the website of the Ministry of Infrastructure and Construction that persons who will estimate the value of real estate for private purposes would not be subject to this fine. However, I believe that an explanation on the website is merely not a legal act and does not provide adequate safeguards in the event of legal doubts. I therefore ask the Minister of Infrastructure and Construction to amend the Real Estate Management Act in order to rule out any doubts regarding the interpretation of these laws. Thank you very much.

The ceremonial funeral of Władysław Koba with a full military ceremony took place on 17 September Ub. R. in Przemyśl. Captain. Koba was only buried in his family tomb after 67 years, next to his wife and daughter. Captain. Władysław Koba was executed on January 31, 1949 in a prison in the castle in Rzeszów. Two other members of the Freedom and Independence Association, Leopold Rząsa and Michał Zygo, died together with him. Their bodies were buried secretly at night in the parish cemetery in Zwięczyca. The burial site was kept secret, the families hadn't known about it for a long time. Who were they that the Communist oppressors and judges sentenced them to death and wanted to erase them from our memory? Władysław Koba was born in 1914 in Jarosław. He graduated from the Infantry Sick School in Komorów. He participated in the September campaign. For his bravery in the defense of the Fortress Modlin was awarded the Cross of Valor. Organized and trained ZWZ-AK platoons in the Jarosław district. He organized many armed and diversionary actions. In 1945, after the dissolution of the AK, he became the commander of the circuit Przemyśl organization. Since the founding of the Association of Freedom and Independence, he has been in charge of this association. At the time of his arrest in 1947, he was the head of the WiN WiN district in Rzeszow. Captain's arrest. Koby is considered to be the symbolic end of WiN structures in south-eastern Poland. For independence activities in 1948 The Rzeszów Military District Court sentenced him to death. Leopold Rząsa, who was born in the Labsianka, was sworn in the ZWZ in 1941. He acted in an interview at the premises of AK Słocin, and after the end of the action he became a deputy intelligence officer of the district of AK Rzeszów. After the war he resumed his studies at the Jagiellonian University, obtained a master's degree in law and became an applicant of the District Court in Rzeszów. In the autumn of 1945, he was re-entered into conspiracy. He was in charge of the WiN Association. He was detained by UB officers in Rzeszów and repressed. He took a failed, even suicide attempt to escape. After almost a year and a half of investigation, he was sentenced to death. Michał Zygo was born in 1917 in Rzeszów. Graduated from petty school, took part in the September campaign. During the German occupation he was a soldier of the ZWZ-AK. By order of the conspiracy authorities, he joined the PPR and infiltrated the ranks of this organization. After entering the Red Army in August 1944, he began working at the Regional Command of MO in Rzeszów. At the instigation of Leopold Rząsa's school colleague, he began his conspiracy activities within the WiN Association. He was the best functioning WiN plug in the safety camera. It was thanks to his information that many conspiracy activists avoided death and repression. Michał Zygo was murdered with a Katyn shot in a prison in Rzeszów on 31 January 1949. His corpse, along with the bodies of Władysław Koba and Leopold Rząsa, was buried in a cemetery in Zwięczyca. On October 20th, we will bring the remains of Leopold Rozsa and Michał Zygi to the resting place in the quarters of the steadfast soldiers in Rzeszów. [[♪ The bell ♪]] We're finally gonna walk our heroes out in a decent way, just like they deserve. It will be another funeral of heroes who have kept their dignity to the end, and it will be another funeral which we restore dignity to the Polish state. Hello and praise the heroes! [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-12-sejm-49-3.u542	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! As every September, I had the privilege of co-organizing the celebrations and attending the celebrations in Cierlicko, at the site of the plane crash of pilots Pebble and Wigura, under the monument commemorating the heroes of the tragic flight. This year, on 11 September, we celebrated the 85th anniversary of the death of Capt. pilot Franciszek Pebble and Eng. Stanislaw Wigura. This is the biggest aviation tragedy that happened in the times of peace in Cieszyński Silesia. To mention the dead airmen, we should mention the history of their tragic death and the successes they have achieved in international aviation. 28 August 1932 was a great day of Polish aviation. Polish airmen, Captain. pilot Franciszek Pebble and Eng. Stanisław Wigura, on a Polish plane RWD-6, won a spectacular victory in Berlin's International Tourist Plane Competition Challenge 1932, defeating more than 40 air crews. In Warsaw more than 100,000 were waiting for our crew to return. People. Polish airmen have become gods. In Berlin, however, Żwirko promised Czech pilots that in September he would fly to them for the festival of aviation. On Sunday, 11 September, RWD-6 took off from the airport in Krakow and flew towards Brno. That's where the landing was supposed to take place. The Avionette passed by Cieszyn and flew over Czechoslovakia, but just abroad she fell into a terrible storm. The airmen decided to return to Poland. Unfortunately, there was a violent storm over the hill Kościelec in Cierlick. With unexpected force, he struck a plane that lost its wing and fell to the ground without steering. The funeral of Pebble and Wigura with the participation of Marshal Józef Piłsudski, President Ignacy Mościcki, hundreds of delegation of airmen and thousands of Warsawans began in the morning of 16 September in the church of St. The cross. Franciszek Żwirko and Stanisław Wigura were buried in the Alley of the Decent in the Powęzkowski Cemetery in Warsaw. Today, in many places you can find traces commemorating excellent airmen. One of them is definitely the place of the plane crash, Cierlicko, where every year we meet at the solemn Mass, followed by celebrations commemorating the tragedy under the monument erected for aviation heroes. Cooperation with the president of the local circle of the Polish Cultural and Educational Union in Cierlick, Mr Tadeusz Smugala, results in an increasing distribution of celebrations, which gather not only Poles, but also Czech citizens. After the war, the place commemorating the death of pilots was renovated, and in 1950 a monument was erected there, which reminds us of death every year, but also about the tragedy of the loss of two wonderful pilots for Polish aviation. It is worth a look at Cierlick around 11 November each year, where these celebrations are held. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]

Therefore, fear in such circumstances is greater than the real state of danger. Our infirmity engulfs circles, strengthened not only by the real power of nature, but also by our helplessness and our expectation that there are servants, others who should act for us. Thank you for the sacrifice and the effort you have made to save the inhabitants of the affected areas and to help them. This is good for both your formations, institutions and organizations, as well as the attitude of the members of those who were fully involved in the relief and repair operation. Thank you very much. In addition to his scientific work, which he was extremely devoted to, as evidenced by his works, lectures, publications that are a source of valuable knowledge, he has been actively involved in social activities since his early years. In high school, as a student, he sold independent leaflets, and then under the subterranean ZSolidarity, he kept and sold banned literature. If we add to this the incredible modesty that characterized your professor, we will get a picture of a man brave, socially mature, faithful to his ideals. These features have resulted in active activities for the introduction of one-mandate electoral districts. He supported the movement with his knowledge, experience, speeches, numerous publications, as well as the organisation of seminars on civic knowledge. Activities to raise public awareness, paying attention to current social problems were a real mission for him, a passion he was enthusiastic about. Unimandate electoral districts implement the constitutional principle that it is society that is sovereign, and allow for greater power to be put into the hands of the nation. Unimandate electoral districts guarantee the simplicity and transparency of elections, as well as the responsibilities of voters. Politicians elected in a system of one-mandate electoral districts are not anonymous, and voters know exactly who is elected from their electoral districts. We're gonna miss your professor, his wisdom, his integrity, his social sensitivity. Why do social conflicts not have their representation in the Sejm, the most important control power, the legislative power? Why do we not see energetic groups there demanding resolutions in the interests of their territories or environments? Why does the conflict in the state not prevent the well-informed and far-sighted Sejm? We see for the time no longer know which, that the Sejm is not a mechanism for the transmission of citizens' expectations and postulates between society and the government in Poland, is not a centre of social mediation. The loud elections of political parties, a market of vanity, followed by a majority of Members falling asleep for four years. Contacts with citizens, but why? This alienation gives rise to the recruitment of Members. Comparisons, proportional electoral ordination. Contrary to some appearances, we choose parties, not people. Here's another piece. There is an electoral ordination that provides a strong bond between a Member and a citizen who forces the responsibility of a Member. This majority ordination, based on one-mandate electoral districts, applied in most democratic countries, which by no means eliminates the parties, only makes them seek the trust of the citizens very much. Its mechanism is simple: in each electoral district, among many candidates, citizens of this district choose to be members of one [[♪ The bell ♪]] The one who got the most votes. A man is chosen, not a party. Thank you.

Not only is writing on paper itself forgotten, but also the whole etiquette and celebration associated with it. Every letter has a face. How many times, having taken a letter into his hand, a man without reason, because he did not expect any bad news, felt fear... His hands tremble and wait for him to open. How many times! Then in a letter like a paper coffin there are almost always words such as dead or something in it cry black tears. On the contrary! Sometimes a letter comes in like a fluttering pigeon, hugging his hands, fondling, smiling and impatient as if he wanted to be opened sooner, because he has a surprising happiness to declare, to shout out a radiant word. Then it always rains on your heart a flame or on your lips like a flower a kiss. Ladies and gentlemen, why don't you stop for a moment and write a letter? Maybe the person we're very close to?
